## `AFG-4721-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Core-Temperature Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Food Cold Chain Temp Logger Audit & Sensory Quality
- **Intro:** Core-Temperature Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Food Cold Chain Temp Logger Audit & Sensory Quality — Pre-Calibration Equipment Verification: Reference sensor accuracy: ±0.1°C at 0°C to 50°C. DAQ input resolution: 0.01°C. Cable insulation resistance: ≥ 100 MΩ at 500 VDC.
- **Key tolerance:** Reference sensor accuracy: ±0.1°C at 0°C to 50°C. DAQ input resolution: 0.01°C. Cable insulation resistance: ≥ 100 MΩ at 500 VDC.
- **Standards:** `ISO 17025:2017`, `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO 22000:2018`, `Afghanistan National Standards Authority (ANSA) Technical Regula`

## `AGO-4721-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** MAP-Packaging Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Specialty Butchery / Seafood Cold Preservation & Cutter
- **Intro:** MAP-Packaging Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Specialty Butchery / Seafood Cold Preservation & Cutter — Cold Room Temperature Verification: Cold room temperature must be -18°C ± 1°C at all three points. Door seal compression gap ≤ 0.5 mm. Air curtain velocity ≥ 2 m/s at door opening.
